## plan_set_open(claim × imm_first)

Unpressed imm_first=F => SyncChain
Unpressed imm_first=T => AsyncImmCross
Fresh imm_first=F => SyncChain
Fresh imm_first=T => AsyncImmCross
Duplicate imm_first=F => SkipAlreadyClaimed
Duplicate imm_first=T => SkipAlreadyClaimed
ConflictEngineWins { reserved: false } imm_first=F => SyncChain
ConflictEngineWins { reserved: false } imm_first=T => AsyncImmCross
ConflictEngineWins { reserved: true } imm_first=F => SyncChain
ConflictEngineWins { reserved: true } imm_first=T => AsyncImmCross
ConflictKept { reserved: false } imm_first=F => SkipAlreadyClaimed
ConflictKept { reserved: false } imm_first=T => SkipAlreadyClaimed
ConflictKept { reserved: true } imm_first=F => SkipAlreadyClaimed
ConflictKept { reserved: true } imm_first=T => SkipAlreadyClaimed

## explicit_press_applied_pair(applied × open × has_press)

applied=? open=F has_press=F => ?
applied=? open=F has_press=T => ?
applied=? open=T has_press=F => ?
applied=? open=T has_press=T => ?
applied=F open=F has_press=F => F
applied=F open=F has_press=T => ?
applied=F open=T has_press=F => F
applied=F open=T has_press=T => F
applied=T open=F has_press=F => T
applied=T open=F has_press=T => T
applied=T open=T has_press=F => T
applied=T open=T has_press=T => ?

effectively_tsf_native=F => T
effectively_tsf_native=T => F
